Equivalent.\n\nI'll rewrite computeLaplacian to take pointers and dimensions, compute with precomputed offsets. Actually simplest and fastest: inside the loop compute row offsets:\n- idx = z*nxy + y*nx + x\n- xp offset = (x+1<nx)?1:0\n- xn = (x>0)?1:0\n- yp row offset = (y+1<ny)?nx:0\n- yn = (y>0)?-nx:0\n- zp = (z+1<nz)?nxy:0\n- zn = (z>0)?-nxy:0\n\nThen laplacian = (c[idx+xp]+c[idx+xn]-2c[idx])*invdx2 + ... \n\nThis is exact same arithmetic order? Still, use division to be safe. It won't be expensive since dx constant. Since dx is runtime constant, division per element is expensive. We could precompute inv values but changes rounding.                           const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) noexcept {\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    const size_t id = z * nxy }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n             const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                cptr[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n\n    // Check for std::isinf(val)) {\n            bad = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, c[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, c[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions\ninline double computeLaplacian(const double* __restrict c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                               const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) noexcept {\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    const size_t id = z * nxy + y * nx + x;\n    const std::ptrdiff_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? 1 : 0;\n    const std::ptrdiff_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(nx) : 0;\n    const std::ptrdiff_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(nxy) : 0;\n    const std::ptrdiff_t xn = (x > 0) ? -1 : 0;\n    const std::ptrdiff_t yn = (y > 0) ? -static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(nx) : 0;\n    const std::ptrdiff_t zn = (z > 0) ? -static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(nxy) : 0;\n    const double cc = c[id];\n\n    const double cxx = (c[id + xp] + c[id + xn] - 2.0 * cc) / (dx * dx);\n    const double cyy = (c[id + yp] + c[id + yn] - 2.0 * cc) / (dy * dy);\n    const double czz = (c[id + zp] + c[id + zn] - 2.0 * cc) / (dz * dz);\n\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const double* __restrict cptr = c.data();\n    double* __restrict muptr = mu.data();\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const double cv = cptr[idx];\n\n                muptr[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                           + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                           - gamma * computeLaplacian(cptr, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,\n                        const std::vector<double>& mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    double* __restrict cnewptr = cnew.data();\n    const double* __restrict coldptr = cold.data();\n    const double* __restrict muptr = mu.data();\n    const double coeff = dt * D;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                cnewptr[idx] = coldptr[idx] + coeff *\n
